command-line interface for controlling a torrent in qBittorrent via its Web API.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt allows you to manage torrents directly from the terminal, supporting operations such as:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdding, resuming, stopping, and removing torrents\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSetting categories, tags, sequential download, and superseeding\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMonitoring torrent upload/download limits, state, and seed time\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInteractive authentication setup or command-line credentials\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBinary runs in Docker\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDesigned to be \u003cstrong\u003ecompact\u003c/strong\u003e, making it easy to compile, deploy, and run.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLightweight, fits on a floppy, yet powerful — perfect for scripting or managing your qBittorrent instance from the \u003cstrong\u003eauth.txt\u003c/strong\u003e (or a file saved with --setup using then using -c filename)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003evia \u003cstrong\u003ecommand-line arguments\u003c/strong\u003e (overides file credentials)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAuth file search order:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e~/.qbtctl/auth.txt\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e./auth.txt\u003c/code\u003e (inside the qbtctl directory)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustom path using \u003ccode\u003e-c \u0026lt;path\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e (overrride)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRun:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"./qbtctl --setup\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e./qbtctl --setup\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYou will be prompted for:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eqBittorrent URL (default: \u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePort (default: 8080)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUsername\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePassword\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNote: To save to program directory just type \u003ccode\u003eauth.txt\u003c/code\u003e or alternate filename, when saving in -i or --setup\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse full filename when saving, it will not create if pointed to a directory only.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default credentials are saved to:       Setup server credentials interactively\n-w, --watch              Watch all torrent stats in real time\n-c \u0026lt;path\u0026gt;                Alternate auth file path\n-r, --raw class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Exit Codes for qbtctl\n0  EXIT_OK            # Success\n1  EXIT_LOGIN_FAIL    # Authentication failed\n2  EXIT_FETCH_FAIL    # API fetch request failed\n3  EXIT_SET_FAIL      # API set request failed\n4  EXIT_BAD_ARGS      # Invalid command-line arguments\n5  EXIT_FILE          # Invalid file operation\n6  EXIT_ACTION        # Action failed \"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Exit Codes for qbtctl\u003c/span\u003e\n0  EXIT_OK            \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Success\u003c/span\u003e\n1  EXIT_LOGIN_FAIL    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Authentication failed\u003c/span\u003e\n2  EXIT_FETCH_FAIL    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e API fetch request failed\u003c/span\u003e\n3  EXIT_SET_FAIL      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e API set request failed\u003c/span\u003e\n4  EXIT_BAD_ARGS      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Invalid command-line arguments\u003c/span\u003e\n5  EXIT_FILE          \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Invalid file operation\u003c/span\u003e\n6  EXIT_ACTION        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Action failed \u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUsage Example:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"./qbtctl -a\nif [ $? -ne 0 ]; then\n    echo \u0026quot;[ERROR] qbtctl encountered an error!\u0026quot;\nfi\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e./qbtctl -a\nif [ $? -ne 0 ]; then\n    echo \"[ERROR] qbtctl encountered an error!\"\nfi\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNotes:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese codes are returned by the CLI when commands directory:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"docker run --rm -v \u0026quot;$(pwd)\u0026quot;:/out alpine:latest /bin/sh -c \u0026quot;\nset -e\n\n# Install build dependencies\napk add --no-cache build-base musl-dev zlib-static pkgconf wget tar libsodium-dev libsodium-static\n\n# Build curl statically\ncd /tmp\nwget -q https://curl.se/download/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz\ntar xzf curl-8.17.0.tar.gz\ncd curl-8.17.0\n./configure --disable-shared --enable-static --without-ssl --disable-ntlm --disable-ldap --disable-ldaps \\\n            --disable-ftp --disable-file --disable-dict --disable-telnet --disable-pop3 --disable-imap \\\n            --disable-smtp --disable-gopher --disable-manual --disable-psl --without-libpsl\nmake -j\\$(nproc)\n\n# Compile all qbtctl sources statically\ncd /out\ngcc -O2 -static -s \\\n-I/tmp/curl-8.17.0/include \\\n-L/tmp/curl-8.17.0/lib/.libs \\\n-o qbtctl qbtctl.c watch.c cJSON.c auth.c help.c \\\n-lcurl -lsodium -lz -lm -ldl -lpthread\n\n# Set ownership to host user\nchown $(id -u):$(id -g) /out/qbtctl\n\n# Cleanup temp files\nrm -rf /tmp/curl-8.17.0 /tmp/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz\n\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003edocker run --rm -v \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$(\u003c/span\u003epwd\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e:/out alpine:latest /bin/sh -c \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eset -e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e# Install build dependencies\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eapk add --no-cache build-base musl-dev zlib-static pkgconf wget tar libsodium-dev libsodium-static\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e# Build curl statically\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecd /tmp\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ewget -q https://curl.se/download/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003etar xzf curl-8.17.0.tar.gz\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecd curl-8.17.0\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e./configure --disable-shared --enable-static --without-ssl --disable-ntlm --disable-ldap --disable-ldaps \u003cspan class=\"pl-cce\"\u003e\\\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e            --disable-ftp --disable-file --disable-dict --disable-telnet --disable-pop3 --disable-imap \u003cspan class=\"pl-cce\"\u003e\\\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e            --disable-smtp --disable-gopher --disable-manual --disable-psl --without-libpsl\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003emake -j\u003cspan class=\"pl-cce\"\u003e\\$\u003c/span\u003e(nproc)\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e# Compile all qbtctl sources statically\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecd /out\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003egcc -O2 -static -s \u003cspan class=\"pl-cce\"\u003e\\\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e-I/tmp/curl-8.17.0/include \u003cspan class=\"pl-cce\"\u003e\\\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan qbtctl","[![Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/creptic/qbtctl)](https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl/releases)","[![Donate](https://img.shields.io/badge/Donate-PayPal-blue.svg)](https://www.paypal.com/paypalme/crepticdev)","![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-yellow.svg)","[![AUR Version](https://img.shields.io/aur/version/qbtctl?label=AUR)](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/qbtctl)","---","","**qbittorrent torrent control** is a minimal, ultra-fast command-line interface for controlling a torrent in qBittorrent via its Web API.","","It allows you to manage torrents directly from the terminal, supporting operations such as:","","- Adding, resuming, stopping, and removing torrents  ","- Setting categories, tags, sequential download, and superseeding ","- Monitoring torrent upload/download limits, state, and seed time  ","- Interactive authentication setup or command-line credentials  ","- Binary runs in Docker","  ","Designed to be **compact**, making it easy to compile, deploy, and run.","","Lightweight, fits on a floppy, yet powerful — perfect for scripting or managing your qBittorrent instance from the terminal.","","## 📥 Download","","Download the latest **precompiled binary (x86 / x86_64 Linux / Docker)**","```bash","wget https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl/releases/latest/download/qbtctl","chmod +x qbtctl","```","https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl/releases/latest/download/qbtctl","","Install from [AUR](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/qbtctl-bin/) with: ``yay qbtctl`` or use ``yay qbtctl-bin`` for binary.  ","","---","","## 💡 **Quick Reference**","","Most common commands and flags are listed in the [**Options**](#options) section below.  ","","---","","# Requirements for Compiling","","Before compiling `qbtctl`, ensure your system has:","","- **C compiler** (`cc`, `gcc`, or `clang`)","- **libcurl** (for connecting to qbittorent api)","- **libsodium** (for encrypting/decrypting password)","- mbedtls (static build only) ","- POSIX-compatible OS (Linux, macOS, etc.)","","Makefile and PKGBUILD are included.","","Compile with: ","","```bash","make","or","make static (optional static portable build)","make install (optional install to /usr/bin, may require sudo)","```","or use PKGBUILD with ``makepkg -i``","","For a portable [**static build using Docker**](#static-build-using-docker), see the Docker section below.","","---","","# Clone make and run","","```bash","git clone https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl.git","cd qbtctl","make","./qbtctl --setup","```","---","","# Authentication Setup","","`qbtctl` requires qBittorrent Web API credentials.","","Credentials can be provided:","","- via **auth.txt** (or a file saved with --setup using then using -c filename)","- via **command-line arguments** (overides file credentials)","","Auth file search order:","","1. `~/.qbtctl/auth.txt`","2. `./auth.txt` (inside the qbtctl directory)","3. Custom path using `-c \u003cpath\u003e` (overrride)","","Run:","","```bash","./qbtctl --setup","```","You will be prompted for:","","- qBittorrent URL (default: `http://localhost`)","- Port (default: 8080)","- Username","- Password","","Note: To save to program directory just type ```auth.txt``` or alternate filename, when saving in -i or --setup","","- Use full filename when saving, it will not create if pointed to a directory only.","      ","By default credentials are saved to: ``~/.qbtctl/auth.txt``","","---","# 💡 Usage Examples","These examples assume you have already set up credentials with `./qbtctl --setup`.","","## 1. Adding a torrent/magnet ","Add a torrent file, set sequential download on, and return name.","```bash","./qbtctl --add torrent.file --set-seqdl 1 -gn","```","Add a magnet link, set ratio 2.0, set seedtime to 4 days","```bash","./qbtctl --add \"magnet:?xt=urn:btih:0123456...\" -srl 2.0 -sst 04:00:00","```","","## 2. Watch all torrents ","Requires a tty, (may scroll spam in some terminals); (Fixed on git)","","Tested and working with Linux TTY and Konsole in Plasma.","```bash","./qbtctl -w","```","","**Example output:**","","```","Active torrents: 3 | Total DL: 28.6M | Total UL: 1.4M","","+-------------------------------------+--------+----------+------+----------+----------+----------+----------+----------+--------------------+--------------+-----------+","|  Name                               | Hash   |     Size | Prog |      ETA |       DL |       UL | Download |   Upload | Tags               | Category     | State     |","+-------------------------------------+--------+----------+------+----------+----------+----------+----------+----------+--------------------+--------------+-----------+","| ubuntu-24.04-desktop-amd64.iso      | 969bd1 |    4.76G |  63% | 00:18:42 |       0B |       0B |    2.76G |    55.1M | Linux, ISO         | linux        | stalledUP |","| archlinux-2026.03.01-x86_64.iso     | 393166 |  895.39M |  92% | 00:02:11 |       0B |       0B |  395.69M |    89.4M | Linux, ISO         | linux        | stalledUP |","| debian-12.10.0-amd64-netinst.iso    | 8d8a89 |    1.06G | 100% | 00:00:00 |       0B |       0B |    1.06G |   312.7M | Linux, ISO         | linux        | stalledUP |","| linuxmint-22-cinnamon-64bit.iso     | 70fa97 |    2.92G |  37% | 00:42:15 |       0B |       0B |    1.92G |    12.4M | Linux, ISO         | linux        | stalledUP |","+-------------------------------------+--------+----------+------+----------+----------+----------+----------+----------+--------------------+--------------+-----------+","|       Press \u003cCtrl\u003e+c to quit        |","+-------------------------------------+","","```","","## 3. Show all torrents","","```bash","./qbtctl -a","```","","```text","","+------------------------------------------+--------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+----------+------------------+","| Name                                     | Hash   | UL Limit(Kb) | DL Limit(Kb) | State        | Seed Time    | Limit D:H:M  | Category | Tags             |","+------------------------------------------+--------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+----------+------------------+","| Ubuntu.ISO                               | 9c3289 | 0            | 0            | stalledUP    | 01:01:01     | 04:04:04     | ISO      | ISO, Debian      |","","```","","With raw output (bytes, seconds, true/false):","","```bash","./qbtctl -r -a","```","","```text","+------------------------------------------+--------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+----------+------------------+","| Name                                     | Hash   | UL Limit (b) | DL Limit (b) | State        | Seed Time    | Limit(secs)  | Category | Tags             |","+------------------------------------------+--------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+----------+------------------+","| Ubuntu.ISO                               | 9c3289 | 0            | 0            | stalledUP    | 90104        | 360240       | ISO      | ISO, Debian      |","","```","","---","","## 4. Show single torrent info (with or without raw)","","```bash","./qbtctl -s -h 9c3289","```","","**Example output:**","","```text","+------------------------------------------+","Name: Ubuntu.ISO","Hash: 9c3289027f9760e0f3fcfd2df40e0d80b2350191","Tags: ISO, Debian","Category: ISO","Upload Limit: 0","Download Limit: 0","Full Path: /incoming/Ubuntu.iso","Ratio Limit: 99.99","Seedtime: 01:01:01","Seedtime Limit: 04:04:04","Sequential Download: 0","Auto TMM: 0","Superseed: 0","Tracker: http://tracker.foo.com","Private: 0","Ratio: 0.20","Upload Speed: 91.50K","Download Speed: 0","Size: 1.98G","Uploaded: 404.07M","Downloaded: 1.98G","ETA: 100:00:00","State: uploading","Progress: 100%","+------------------------------------------+","","```","","With raw JSON:","","```bash","./qbtctl -sj -h 1562EG","```","","```json","{\"name\":\"ARCH.ISO\",\"state\":\"stalledUP\",\"dl_limit\":1048576,\"ul_limit\":524288,\"ratio\":2.0,\"seed_time\":45296,....etc}","```","","---","","## 5. Modify torrent settings","","Set category and tags:","","```bash","./qbtctl -sc Linux -st Linux,ISO -h 9c3289","```","","Set upload and download limits:","","```bash","./qbtctl -sul 1024 -sdl 2048 -h 9c3289","```","","Enable sequential download and superseed:","","```bash","./qbtctl -ssd 1 -sss 1 -h 9c3289","```","","---","","## 6. Pause(stop), resume(start), or remove torrents","","Pause a torrent:","","```bash","./qbtctl -ap -h 9c3289","```","","Resume a torrent:","","```bash","./qbtctl -as -h 9c3289","```","","Remove torrent (keep data):","","```bash","./qbtctl -ar -h 9c3289","```","","Delete torrent and data:","","```bash","./qbtctl -del -h 9c3289","```","","---","","## 7. Move torrent data","","```bash","./qbtctl -am /downloads/linux -h 9c3289","```","","Moves the files to `/downloads/linux` on the server.","","---","","## 8. Quick non-interactive run","","If you want to run commands directly with credentials:","","```bash","./qbtctl --url http://localhost:8080 --user admin --pass mypass -a","```","---","","# Options","","```bash","-help, --help            Show help message","-i, --setup              Setup server credentials interactively","-w, --watch              Watch all torrent stats in real time","-c \u003cpath\u003e                Alternate auth file path","-r, --raw                Raw output mode (bytes, seconds, true/false)","--url \u003curl\u003e              qBittorrent WebUI URL (ex: http://localhost:8080)","--user \u003cuser\u003e            qBittorrent username (default: admin)","--pass \u003cpass\u003e            qBittorrent password (NULL allowed)","-h, --hash \u003chash\u003e        Torrent hash (minimum 6 characters)","-a, --show-all           Show basic torrent info for all torrents","-c, --show-all-clean     Show torrents info (borderless output)","-aj, --show-all-json     Show all torrents info as JSON","-s, --show-single        Show single torrent info (requires hash)","-sj, --show-single-json  Show single torrent info as JSON (requires hash)","-v, --version            Show version and exit","```","","---","","# Actions (requires --hash)","","```bash","-am \u003cpath\u003e, --move \u003cpath\u003e  Move torrent to \u003cpath\u003e on the server","-as, --start               Start torrent","-af, --force-start         Force start torrent","-ap, --pause               Pause or stop torrent","-ar, --remove              Remove torrent (keeps data)","-del, --delete             Remove, and delete torrent and data","-add \u003cfile/magnet\u003e, --add \u003cfile/magnet\u003e Add torrent via file or magnet link","```","","---","# Setters (requires --hash)","```bash","# Command                             Standard Input  --raw Input","-sc \u003ccat\u003e, --set-category \u003ccat\u003e       Linux           Linux","-st \u003ctag\u003e, --set-tags \u003ctag\u003e           Linux,ISO       Linux,ISO","-sul \u003c#\u003e, --set-up-limit \u003c#\u003e          512 (KB/s)      524288","-sdl \u003c#\u003e, --set-dl-limit \u003c#\u003e          1024 (KB/s)     1048576","-srl \u003c#\u003e, --set-ratio-limit \u003c#\u003e       2.0             2.0","-sst \u003c#\u003e, --set-seedtime-limit \u003c#\u003e    24:00:00        86400","-ssd \u003cval\u003e, --set-seqdl \u003cval\u003e         1 (ON)          true","-sat \u003cval\u003e, --set-autotmm \u003cval\u003e       0 (OFF)         false","-sss \u003cval\u003e, --set-superseed \u003cval\u003e     0 (OFF)         false","```","---","# Getters (requires --hash)","","```bash","# Command                   Standard Output              --raw Output (bytes/secs/boolean)","-gn, --get-name             Ubuntu.ISO                   \"Ubuntu.ISO\"","-gt, --get-tags             Linux,ISO                    \"Linux, ISO\"","-gc, --get-category         Linux ISOs                   \"Linux ISOs\"","-gu, --get-up-limit         512K (Kb/Mb/Gb)              524288","-gd, --get-dl-limit         1024K (Kb/Mb/Gb)             1048576","-gdp, --get-dl-path         /downloads/linux/iso.iso     \"/downloads/linux/iso.iso\"","-gr, --get-ratio-limit      2.0                          2.0","-gs, --get-seedtime         12:34:56                     45296","-gsl,--get-seedtime-limit   24:00:00                     86400","-gsd, --get-seqdl           0 (OFF)                      false","-gat, --get-autotmm         0 (OFF)                      false","-gss, --get-superseed       1 (ON)                       true","-gtr, --get-tracker         tracker.example.com          \"tracker.example.com:port/announce\"","-gtl, --get-tracker-list    tracker1,tracker2            \"udp.tracker1:port\",\"tracker2:port\"","-gp, --get-private          1 (YES)                      true","-gr, --get-ratio            0.2                          0.321596","-gus, --get-up-speed        3.65K (Kb/Mb/Gb)             3618 ","-gds, --get-dl-speed        0 (Kb/Mb/Gb)                 0","-gsz, --get-size            1.98G (Kb/Mb/Gb)             2125170914","-gud, --get-uploaded        654.17M (Kb/Mb/Gb)           85946796","-gdd, --get-downloaded      1.98G                        2126201670","-ge, --get-eta              100:00:00 (100:00:00=inf.)   8640000","-gst, --get-status          ForcedUP                     \"ForcedUP\"","-gpr, --get-progress        100%                         1.000000","-ghl, --get-hash-list       9c3289,012345                9c3289000000000etc,012345678*","```","---","## ⚠ Exit / Error Codes","","```bash","# Exit Codes for qbtctl","0  EXIT_OK            # Success","1  EXIT_LOGIN_FAIL    # Authentication failed","2  EXIT_FETCH_FAIL    # API fetch request failed","3  EXIT_SET_FAIL      # API set request failed","4  EXIT_BAD_ARGS      # Invalid command-line arguments","5  EXIT_FILE          # Invalid file operation","6  EXIT_ACTION        # Action failed ","``` ","Usage Example:","```","./qbtctl -a","if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then","    echo \"[ERROR] qbtctl encountered an error!\"","fi","```","Notes:","","These codes are returned by the CLI when commands fail.","","---","","# Static Build Using Docker","","Run this inside the source directory:","","```bash","docker run --rm -v \"$(pwd)\":/out alpine:latest /bin/sh -c \"","set -e","","# Install build dependencies","apk add --no-cache build-base musl-dev zlib-static pkgconf wget tar libsodium-dev libsodium-static","","# Build curl statically","cd /tmp","wget -q https://curl.se/download/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz","tar xzf curl-8.17.0.tar.gz","cd curl-8.17.0","./configure --disable-shared --enable-static --without-ssl --disable-ntlm --disable-ldap --disable-ldaps \\","            --disable-ftp --disable-file --disable-dict --disable-telnet --disable-pop3 --disable-imap \\","            --disable-smtp --disable-gopher --disable-manual --disable-psl --without-libpsl","make -j\\$(nproc)","","# Compile all qbtctl sources statically","cd /out","gcc -O2 -static -s \\","-I/tmp/curl-8.17.0/include \\","-L/tmp/curl-8.17.0/lib/.libs \\","-o qbtctl qbtctl.c watch.c cJSON.c auth.c help.c \\","-lcurl -lsodium -lz -lm -ldl -lpthread","","# Set ownership to host user","chown $(id -u):$(id -g) /out/qbtctl","","# Cleanup temp files","rm -rf /tmp/curl-8.17.0 /tmp/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz","\"","```","---","","# ☕ Support / Donate ","If you enjoy **qbtctl**, consider buying me a coffee","","[![Donate](https://img.shields.io/badge/Donate-PayPal-blue.svg)](https://www.paypal.com/paypalme/crepticdev)","","---","# Notes","- No https support. <p dir="auto"><strong>qbittorrent torrent control</strong> is a minimal, ultra-fast command-line interface for controlling a torrent in qBittorrent via its Web API.</p>
<p dir="auto">It allows you to manage torrents directly from the terminal, supporting operations such as:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Adding, resuming, stopping, and removing torrents</li>
<li>Setting categories, tags, sequential download, and superseeding</li>
<li>Monitoring torrent upload/download limits, state, and seed time</li>
<li>Interactive authentication setup or command-line credentials</li>
<li>Binary runs in Docker</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Designed to be <strong>compact</strong>, making it easy to compile, deploy, and run.</p>
<p dir="auto">Lightweight, fits on a floppy, yet powerful — perfect for scripting or managing your qBittorrent instance from the terminal.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📥 Download</h2><a id="user-content--download" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📥 Download" href="#-download"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Download the latest <strong>precompiled binary (x86 / x86_64 Linux / Docker)</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="wget https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl/releases/latest/download/qbtctl
chmod +x qbtctl"><pre>wget https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl/releases/latest/download/qbtctl
chmod +x qbtctl</pre></div>

<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💡 <strong>Quick Reference</strong></h2><a id="user-content--quick-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💡 Quick Reference" href="#-quick-reference"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Most common commands and flags are listed in the <a href="#options"><strong>Options</strong></a> section below.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements for Compiling</h1><a id="user-content-requirements-for-compiling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements for Compiling" href="#requirements-for-compiling"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Before compiling <code>qbtctl</code>, ensure your system has:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>C compiler</strong> (<code>cc</code>, <code>gcc</code>, or <code>clang</code>)</li>
<li><strong>libcurl</strong> (for connecting to qbittorent api)</li>
<li><strong>libsodium</strong> (for encrypting/decrypting password)</li>
<li>mbedtls (static build only)</li>
<li>POSIX-compatible OS (Linux, macOS, etc.)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Makefile and PKGBUILD are included.</p>
<p dir="auto">Compile with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make
or
make static (optional static portable build)
make install (optional install to /usr/bin, may require sudo)"><pre>make
or
make static (optional static portable build)
make install (optional install to /usr/bin, may require sudo)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">or use PKGBUILD with <code>makepkg -i</code></p>
<p dir="auto">For a portable <a href="#static-build-using-docker"><strong>static build using Docker</strong></a>, see the Docker section below.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Clone make and run</h1><a id="user-content-clone-make-and-run" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Clone make and run" href="#clone-make-and-run"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

cd qbtctl
make
./qbtctl --setup"><pre>git clone https://github.com/creptic/qbtctl.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> qbtctl
make
./qbtctl --setup</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Authentication Setup</h1><a id="user-content-authentication-setup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Authentication Setup" href="#authentication-setup"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>qbtctl</code> requires qBittorrent Web API credentials.</p>
<p dir="auto">Credentials can be provided:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>via <strong>auth.txt</strong> (or a file saved with --setup using then using -c filename)</li>
<li>via <strong>command-line arguments</strong> (overides file credentials)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Auth file search order:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><code>~/.qbtctl/auth.txt</code></li>
<li><code>./auth.txt</code> (inside the qbtctl directory)</li>
<li>Custom path using <code>-c &lt;path&gt;</code> (overrride)</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl --setup"><pre>./qbtctl --setup</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You will be prompted for:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>qBittorrent URL (default: <code>http://localhost</code>)</li>
<li>Port (default: 8080)</li>
<li>Username</li>
<li>Password</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Note: To save to program directory just type <code>auth.txt</code> or alternate filename, when saving in -i or --setup</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Use full filename when saving, it will not create if pointed to a directory only.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">By default credentials are saved to: <code>~/.qbtctl/auth.txt</code></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💡 Usage Examples</h1><a id="user-content--usage-examples" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💡 Usage Examples" href="#-usage-examples"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">These examples assume you have already set up credentials with <code>./qbtctl --setup</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Adding a torrent/magnet</h2><a id="user-content-1-adding-a-torrentmagnet" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Adding a torrent/magnet" href="#1-adding-a-torrentmagnet"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Add a torrent file, set sequential download on, and return name.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl --add torrent.file --set-seqdl 1 -gn"><pre>./qbtctl --add torrent.file --set-seqdl 1 -gn</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Add a magnet link, set ratio 2.0, set seedtime to 4 days</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl --add &quot;magnet:?xt=urn:btih:0123456...&quot; -srl 2.0 -sst 04:00:00"><pre>./qbtctl --add <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>magnet:?xt=urn:btih:0123456...<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> -srl 2.0 -sst 04:00:00</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Watch all torrents</h2><a id="user-content-2-watch-all-torrents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Watch all torrents" href="#2-watch-all-torrents"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Requires a tty, (may scroll spam in some terminals); (Fixed on git)</p>
<p dir="auto">Tested and working with Linux TTY and Konsole in Plasma.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -w"><pre>./qbtctl -w</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Show all torrents</h2><a id="user-content-3-show-all-torrents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Show all torrents" href="#3-show-all-torrents"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -a"><pre>./qbtctl -a</pre></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="

"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>

| Ubuntu.ISO                               | 9c3289 | 0            | 0            | stalledUP    | 01:01:01     | 04:04:04     | ISO      | ISO, Debian      |

</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">With raw output (bytes, seconds, true/false):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -r -a"><pre>./qbtctl -r -a</pre></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="+------------------------------------------+--------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+--------------+----------+------------------+

| Name                                     | Hash   | UL Limit (b) | DL Limit (b) | State        | Seed Time    | Limit(secs)  | Category | Tags             |

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Show single torrent info (with or without raw)</h2><a id="user-content-4-show-single-torrent-info-with-or-without-raw" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Show single torrent info (with or without raw)" href="#4-show-single-torrent-info-with-or-without-raw"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -s -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -s -h 9c3289</pre></div>

<p dir="auto">With raw JSON:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -sj -h 1562EG"><pre>./qbtctl -sj -h 1562EG</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;ARCH.ISO&quot;,&quot;state&quot;:&quot;stalledUP&quot;,&quot;dl_limit&quot;:1048576,&quot;ul_limit&quot;:524288,&quot;ratio&quot;:2.0,&quot;seed_time&quot;:45296,....etc}"><pre>{<span class="pl-ent">"name"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ARCH.ISO<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"state"</span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>stalledUP<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,<span class="pl-ent">"dl_limit"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">1048576</span>,<span class="pl-ent">"ul_limit"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">524288</span>,<span class="pl-ent">"ratio"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">2.0</span>,<span class="pl-ent">"seed_time"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">45296</span>,<span class="pl-ii">....etc</span>}</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5. Modify torrent settings</h2><a id="user-content-5-modify-torrent-settings" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5. Modify torrent settings" href="#5-modify-torrent-settings"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Set category and tags:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -sc Linux -st Linux,ISO -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -sc Linux -st Linux,ISO -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Set upload and download limits:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -sul 1024 -sdl 2048 -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -sul 1024 -sdl 2048 -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Enable sequential download and superseed:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -ssd 1 -sss 1 -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -ssd 1 -sss 1 -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6. Pause(stop), resume(start), or remove torrents</h2><a id="user-content-6-pausestop-resumestart-or-remove-torrents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6. Pause(stop), resume(start), or remove torrents" href="#6-pausestop-resumestart-or-remove-torrents"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Pause a torrent:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -ap -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -ap -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Resume a torrent:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -as -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -as -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Remove torrent (keep data):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -ar -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -ar -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Delete torrent and data:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -del -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -del -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">7. Move torrent data</h2><a id="user-content-7-move-torrent-data" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7. Move torrent data" href="#7-move-torrent-data"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -am /downloads/linux -h 9c3289"><pre>./qbtctl -am /downloads/linux -h 9c3289</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Moves the files to <code>/downloads/linux</code> on the server.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">8. Quick non-interactive run</h2><a id="user-content-8-quick-non-interactive-run" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 8. Quick non-interactive run" href="#8-quick-non-interactive-run"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you want to run commands directly with credentials:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl --url http://localhost:8080 --user admin --pass mypass -a"><pre>./qbtctl --url http://localhost:8080 --user admin --pass mypass -a</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Options</h1><a id="user-content-options" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Options" href="#options"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="-help, --help            Show help message
-i, --setup              Setup server credentials interactively
-w, --watch              Watch all torrent stats in real time
-c &lt;path&gt;                Alternate auth file path
-r, --raw                Raw output mode (bytes, seconds, true/false)
--url &lt;url&gt;              qBittorrent WebUI URL (ex: http://localhost:8080)
--user &lt;user&gt;            qBittorrent username (default: admin)
--pass &lt;pass&gt;            qBittorrent password (NULL allowed)
-h, --hash &lt;hash&gt;        Torrent hash (minimum 6 characters)
-a, --show-all           Show basic torrent info for all torrents
-c, --show-all-clean     Show torrents info (borderless output)
-aj, --show-all-json     Show all torrents info as JSON
-s, --show-single        Show single torrent info (requires hash)
-sj, --show-single-json  Show single torrent info as JSON (requires hash)
-v, --version            Show version and exit"><pre>-help, --help            Show <span class="pl-c1">help</span> message
-i, --setup              Setup server credentials interactively
-w, --watch              Watch all torrent stats <span class="pl-k">in</span> real <span class="pl-k">time</span>
-c <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>                Alternate auth file path
-r, --raw                Raw output mode (bytes, seconds, true/false)
--url <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>url<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>              qBittorrent WebUI URL (ex: http://localhost:8080)
--user <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>user<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>            qBittorrent username (default: admin)
--pass <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>pass<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>            qBittorrent password (NULL allowed)
-h, --hash <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>hash<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>        Torrent <span class="pl-c1">hash</span> (minimum 6 characters)
-a, --show-all           Show basic torrent info <span class="pl-k">for</span> all torrents
-c, --show-all-clean     Show torrents info (borderless output)
-aj, --show-all-json     Show all torrents info as JSON
-s, --show-single        Show single torrent info (requires hash)
-sj, --show-single-json  Show single torrent info as JSON (requires hash)
-v, --version            Show version and <span class="pl-c1">exit</span></pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Actions (requires --hash)</h1><a id="user-content-actions-requires---hash" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Actions (requires --hash)" href="#actions-requires---hash"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="-am &lt;path&gt;, --move &lt;path&gt;  Move torrent to &lt;path&gt; on the server
-as, --start               Start torrent
-af, --force-start         Force start torrent
-ap, --pause               Pause or stop torrent
-ar, --remove              Remove torrent (keeps data)
-del, --delete             Remove, and delete torrent and data
-add &lt;file/magnet&gt;, --add &lt;file/magnet&gt; Add torrent via file or magnet link"><pre>-am <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>, --move <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>  Move torrent to <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>path<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> on the server
-as, --start               Start torrent
-af, --force-start         Force start torrent
-ap, --pause               Pause or stop torrent
-ar, --remove              Remove torrent (keeps data)
-del, --delete             Remove, and delete torrent and data
-add <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>file/magnet<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>, --add <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>file/magnet<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> Add torrent via file or magnet link</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Setters (requires --hash)</h1><a id="user-content-setters-requires---hash" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Setters (requires --hash)" href="#setters-requires---hash"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">⚠ Exit / Error Codes</h2><a id="user-content--exit--error-codes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ⚠ Exit / Error Codes" href="#-exit--error-codes"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Exit Codes for qbtctl
0  EXIT_OK            # Success
1  EXIT_LOGIN_FAIL    # Authentication failed
2  EXIT_FETCH_FAIL    # API fetch request failed
3  EXIT_SET_FAIL      # API set request failed
4  EXIT_BAD_ARGS      # Invalid command-line arguments
5  EXIT_FILE          # Invalid file operation
6  EXIT_ACTION        # Action failed "><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Exit Codes for qbtctl</span>
0  EXIT_OK            <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Success</span>
1  EXIT_LOGIN_FAIL    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Authentication failed</span>
2  EXIT_FETCH_FAIL    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> API fetch request failed</span>
3  EXIT_SET_FAIL      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> API set request failed</span>
4  EXIT_BAD_ARGS      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Invalid command-line arguments</span>
5  EXIT_FILE          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Invalid file operation</span>
6  EXIT_ACTION        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Action failed </span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Usage Example:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="./qbtctl -a
if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    echo &quot;[ERROR] qbtctl encountered an error!&quot;
fi"><pre class="notranslate"><code>./qbtctl -a
if [ $? -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "[ERROR] qbtctl encountered an error!"
fi
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Notes:</p>
<p dir="auto">These codes are returned by the CLI when commands fail.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Static Build Using Docker</h1><a id="user-content-static-build-using-docker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Static Build Using Docker" href="#static-build-using-docker"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run this inside the source directory:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run --rm -v &quot;$(pwd)&quot;:/out alpine:latest /bin/sh -c &quot;
set -e

# Install build dependencies
apk add --no-cache build-base musl-dev zlib-static pkgconf wget tar libsodium-dev libsodium-static

# Build curl statically
cd /tmp
wget -q https://curl.se/download/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz
tar xzf curl-8.17.0.tar.gz
cd curl-8.17.0
./configure --disable-shared --enable-static --without-ssl --disable-ntlm --disable-ldap --disable-ldaps \
            --disable-ftp --disable-file --disable-dict --disable-telnet --disable-pop3 --disable-imap \
            --disable-smtp --disable-gopher --disable-manual --disable-psl --without-libpsl
make -j\$(nproc)

# Compile all qbtctl sources statically
cd /out
gcc -O2 -static -s \
-I/tmp/curl-8.17.0/include \
-L/tmp/curl-8.17.0/lib/.libs \
-o qbtctl qbtctl.c watch.c cJSON.c auth.c help.c \
-lcurl -lsodium -lz -lm -ldl -lpthread

# Set ownership to host user
chown $(id -u):$(id -g) /out/qbtctl

# Cleanup temp files
rm -rf /tmp/curl-8.17.0 /tmp/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz
&quot;"><pre>docker run --rm -v <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>pwd<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>:/out alpine:latest /bin/sh -c <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">set -e</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s"># Install build dependencies</span>
<span class="pl-s">apk add --no-cache build-base musl-dev zlib-static pkgconf wget tar libsodium-dev libsodium-static</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s"># Build curl statically</span>
<span class="pl-s">cd /tmp</span>
<span class="pl-s">wget -q https://curl.se/download/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz</span>
<span class="pl-s">tar xzf curl-8.17.0.tar.gz</span>
<span class="pl-s">cd curl-8.17.0</span>
<span class="pl-s">./configure --disable-shared --enable-static --without-ssl --disable-ntlm --disable-ldap --disable-ldaps <span class="pl-cce">\</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">            --disable-ftp --disable-file --disable-dict --disable-telnet --disable-pop3 --disable-imap <span class="pl-cce">\</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">            --disable-smtp --disable-gopher --disable-manual --disable-psl --without-libpsl</span>
<span class="pl-s">make -j<span class="pl-cce">\$</span>(nproc)</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s"># Compile all qbtctl sources statically</span>
<span class="pl-s">cd /out</span>
<span class="pl-s">gcc -O2 -static -s <span class="pl-cce">\</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">-I/tmp/curl-8.17.0/include <span class="pl-cce">\</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">-L/tmp/curl-8.17.0/lib/.libs <span class="pl-cce">\</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">-o qbtctl qbtctl.c watch.c cJSON.c auth.c help.c <span class="pl-cce">\</span></span>
<span class="pl-s">-lcurl -lsodium -lz -lm -ldl -lpthread</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s"># Set ownership to host user</span>
<span class="pl-s">chown <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>id -u<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span>:<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>id -g<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span> /out/qbtctl</span>
<span class="pl-s"></span>
<span class="pl-s"># Cleanup temp files</span>
<span class="pl-s">rm -rf /tmp/curl-8.17.0 /tmp/curl-8.17.0.tar.gz</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
